eṇvaki uṇavu	-	payaṟu, uḻuntu, kaṭuku, kaṭali, eḷ, 			koḷḷu, avari, tuvari (marapu: 			78) iḷam, - nel, kāṇam, varaku, 				iṟuṅku, tiṉi, cāmi, pullu, 				kōtumi (marapu: 79)
eṉac coṟkum, tokiccoṟkum poruḷ viḷakkam tarukiṟār.
ca. kurucāmi

6.  ilakkaṇattil etu iṭampeṟa vēṇṭum?
karuttaraṅkat talippu, kēḷvi uruvattil iruppiṉum palavakiyāṉa viḷakkaṅkaḷukku iṭam taruvatāka amin tuḷḷatu. karuttaraṅkap poruḷ attakiyatāka irukkalām; allatu kēḷvi amippu attakiya pala poruḷ viḷakkattiṟku iṭam tantirukkalām.
oru moḻiyiṉ colluruvaṅkaḷiṉ aṭippaṭiyil ilakkaṇam oṉṟu uruvākumpōtu atil vitikaḷum, vitivilakkukaḷum iṭam peṟum. ilakkaṇatti variyaṟi ceykiṉṟavarkaḷ avvāṟutāṉ kōṭiṭṭuk kāṭṭuvar. ittakiya ilakkaṇattil iru mukaṅkaḷuṇṭu; 1. moḻic colluruvam ākiya ataṉ uḷḷīṭu  2. potumiyāṉa vitikaḷum ciṟappu vitikaḷum aṭaṅkiya ilakkaṇa amippumuṟi eṉpaṉa ataṉ iru mukaṅkaḷ. maṟṟoru vaḻiyil coṉṉāl vitikaḷum coṟpaṭṭiyalkaḷum aṭaṅkiyavi ilakkaṇam eṉṟu kūṟa iyalum. mutal mukatti uḷḷīṭu eṉṟum iraṇṭāvatu mukatti amippu nili eṉṟum nām peyariṭṭuk koḷḷalām.
`etu eṉṟa kēḷvik kuṟiyīṭu, uḷḷīṭṭik kuṟikkalām; allatu, amippu niliyik kuṟikkalām. oru moḻiyiṉ colluruvam aḷavaṟṟup parantu kiṭappatu; palatarappaṭṭatu. avaṟṟuḷ evi evi uḷḷīṭāka amiya vēṇṭum eṉṟu poruḷ ceytukoṇṭāl colluruvam maṭṭum paṟṟiya kēḷviyāka atu māṟum, ataṉic caṟṟu viḷakka vēṇṭum.
ōr ilakkaṇam ellā moḻi uruvaṅkaḷiyum uḷḷaṭakki uruvāvatu; allatu pōtumāṉa colluru vaṅkaḷi uḷḷaṭakkuvatu; allatu cila colluruvaṅkaḷi maṭṭum uḷḷaṭakkuvatu eṉṟu mūṉṟāka vēṟupaṭuttiṭa muṭiyum. oru moḻiyiṉ ellāc col uruvaṅkaḷiyum muṉṉar eḻunta, ippōtu eḻukiṉṟa, varuṅkālattil eḻum uruvaṅkaḷiyum uḷḷaṭakkuvatu iyalāta amippākum. ēṉiṉal antac colluruvaṅkaḷ palatarappaṭṭaviyāka irukkum. anta ilakkaṇattiṉ uḷḷīṭāka vaḻakkukaḷiyum irumoḻi pēcuvōriṉ pēcciyum uṭkoṇṭāl vitikaḷ potuvāka amiyātu. taṉic coṟkaḷik kuṟippatākap perumpālum amiyum. maliyāḷa ilakkaṇa āciriyar ē.ār. irāja irāja varmā taṉatu kēraḷa pāṇiṉīyattil camakiruta uruvaṅkaḷukkum ilakkaṇa viti amikka muṟpaṭṭatāl potu vitikaḷaṉittum ciṟappu vitikaḷāka, coṟpaṭṭiyalkaḷāka māṟiṉa. eṉavē oru moḻiyiṉ ellāc colluruvaṅkaḷiyum uḷḷīṭākak koṇṭāl ilakkaṇam coṟpaṭṭikiyāka māṟum; potu vitikaḷi vakukka iyalātu.
ataṟku māṟākap pōtiya colluruvaṅkaḷi maṭṭum ōr ilakkaṇam aṭippaṭiyākak koṇṭāl, amippu muṟiyic cemmiyāka amittiṭa muṭiyum. atu aṭippaṭi yākak koḷḷum collīṭukaḷ tērnteṭukkappaṭṭaviyāka irukkum. inta muṟiyip piṉpaṟṟiṉāl maṟṟoru cikkal eḻum. ilakkaṇattil uḷḷīṭāka aminta celluruvam pōtumāṉatā eṉṟu muṭipu ceyvatu evvāṟu? eṉpatē antac cikkal. ēṟattāḻa mūvāyiram vākkiyaṅkaḷik koṇṭa uḷḷīṭṭi aṭippaṭiyākak koṇṭu vakukkappaṭṭa vitikaḷ, ammoḻi uruvaṅkaḷ aṉittiyum viḷakkukiṉṟaṉavā eṉṟu cōtikkalām. oru moḻiyiṉ uḷḷīṭu pōtumāṉatā eṉṟu aṟivataṟku, oru kuṟippiṭṭa aḷavilāṉa vākkiyaṅ kaḷittām aṭippaṭiyākak koṇṭuḷḷōm. anta vākkiyaṅkaḷiṉ tokuppu variyaṟiyaṟṟataṉṟu. tiṭṭamāṉatu, iraṇṭāyiram allatu mūvāyiram eṉṟa eṇṇikki aḷavu peṟṟatu.
āyirattut toḷḷāyirattu aimpatil vāḻnta moḻiyiyalāḷarukkut tiṭṭamāṉa colluruvaṅkaḷ, tappip piḻikkum oruvaḻi niliyāka iruntatu. yārēṉum, oru vitikku māṟupaṭṭa colluruvaṅkaḷ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa eṉṟu kuraleḻuppiṉāl, `eṉatu uḷḷīṭṭu vākkiyaṅkaḷil antac colluruvaṅkaḷ kāṇappaṭavilli eṉṟu camātāṉam kūṟi  naḻuviṉar. appōtellām tappip piḻikkum anta vaḻiyi mēṟkoḷvar. `anta uruvam eṉatu vākkiya uḷḷīṭṭil illi allatu `eṉatu kaḷāyvuk kuṟippil antac colluruvamilli eṉṟu kūṟit tappiṉar. ilakkaṇa vitikaḷukku etir uruvaṅkaḷ iruppati amippiyalāḷarkaḷ (ṭrakcuraliṭ) cuṭṭikkāṭṭiṉar. eṉavē ilakkaṇattiṉ iyalpi variyaṟi ceyvatu vari, ettakiya col luruvaṅkaḷ ilakkaṇattil iṭampeṟa vēṇṭum eṉṟu tiṭṭamākak kūṟāmal iruntatipa perum kuṟiyāka moḻiyiyalāḷarkaḷ karutavilli.
intiya ilakkaṇa āciriyarkaḷaṉivarum uḷḷīṭṭuc colluruvaṅkaḷiṉ kuṟikaḷi uṇarntiruntaṉar. ataṉāl tāṉ tamatu ilakkaṇa vitikaḷiṉ iṟutiyil `aṟiñarkaḷiṉ payaṉpāṭṭi nōkkit terintiṭuka allatu `aṟiñarkaḷiṭam kēṭṭut terintu koḷka eṉṟa cūttiramum amittiruk kiṉṟaṉar. `cēṣam pirayōkātñēyam (iṅkuk kūṟātaṉa vaṟṟip payaṉpāṭṭāl terintukoḷka); `vallār vāyk kēṭṭuṇarka (iṅkuk kūṟātavaṟṟi aṟiñarkaḷ pāl kēṭṭuṇarka) pōṉṟa cūttiraṅkaḷ allatu viḷakkaṅkaḷ maliyāḷattilum tamiḻilum marapuvaḻipapaṭṭa ilakkaṇaṅkaḷil kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.
mūṉṟāvatu kūṟiya kuṟinta aḷavilāṉa uḷḷīṭu, oru kuṟippiṭṭa ilakkaṇap pakuppiṟkup payaṉpaṭuttumpōtu, pōtiyatāka amiyiṉum motta ilakkaṇattiṟku atu aḷaviṟ kuṟintatākavē teṉpaṭum. piṟaviṉi uruvaṅ kaḷaṉittiyum uḷḷīṭāka amiyum ilakkaṇattiṟku vākkiya uruvaṅkaḷ aṉittiyum atu aṭippaṭiyākak koṇṭālum motta ilakkaṇattiṟku anta vākkiya uḷḷīṭukaḷ pōtumāṉaviyāka amiyā. pāṭañcollum vakuppukaḷil payaṉpaṭum eṭuttukkāṭṭu uruvaṅkaḷi maṭṭum koṇṭa ilakkaṇam mutirāta kuḻanti niliyiluḷḷa ilakkaṇamāka amiyum. yārum ataṉit takka ilakkaṇamākak karuta māṭṭārkaḷ. eṉavē mūṉṟāvatu viḷakkatti nām nirākarittuviṭalām.
iraṇṭāvatu piriviṟ kūṟiya ilakkaṇattiṉ amippup paṟṟi ituvari nāṉ etuvum kūṟavilli. ellā ilakkaṇaṅkaḷilum potu vitikaḷilum ciṟappu vitikaḷum kāṇappaṭum. maṟṟoru vaḻiyil coṉṉāl ovvoru ilakkaṇattilum vitikaḷum coṟkaḷiṉ paṭṭiyalum kāṇappaṭum. uḷḷīṭṭuc coṟkaḷ perukap peruka potu vitikaḷ kuṟintu ciṟappu vitikaḷ perukum. ciṟappu vitikaḷ allatu coṟpaṭṭiyalik kuṟikka, uḷḷīṭṭuc coṟkaḷi aṟiñarkaḷ kuṟikka muyalvar. `avi taraṅ kuṟintavi allatu `cīriya aṟiñarkaḷuṭiya pēccō allatu kēḷviyō alla eṉṟu avaṟṟi nirākarittuviṭuvatu oru vaḻi. piramāṇa ilakkaṇamāka (reḵpareṉ kirāmar) iraṇṭu aṇmiyil veḷivantuḷḷaṉa. atil oṉṟu, cāmuval mārṭṭiṉ `cappāṉ moḻiyiṉ piramāṇa ilakkaṇam (roḵpareṉ kirāmar āḵp jāpaṉī) eṉṟa nūlil, potu vitikaḷum coṟpaṭṭiyalum tantirukkiṉṟār. vitikku etirāṉa coṟkaḷi varuki muṟikku ēṟpak koṭuttirukkiṉṟār. āṭṭō yepparcaṉ eḻutiya `āṅkila ilakkaṇattiṉ mukkiya amcam (ecaṉciyal āḵp iṅkilī kirāmar) eṉṟa nūl iraṇṭāvatu nūlākum. anta nūlilum potu vitikaḷum avaṟṟiṟku etirāṉa eṭuttukkāṭṭukaḷum tarappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. eṉavē perukiya uḷḷīṭṭāl potu vitikaḷum etirāṉa coṟpaṭṭiyalumtām koṭuttiṭa muṭiyum eṉpatu teḷivākiṉṟaṉa. puṟaniliyil (carḵpēḵ leval) avvāṟu tāṉ ceyya iyalum. ellāc coṟkaḷiyum viti mūlam tōṟṟuvittiṭa muṭiyātu eṉpatu uṟutiyākiṟatu. akanili amippi (ṭīpleval) kaṇakkileṭuttu vitiyamittāl, kūṭutalaḷavil moḻi uruvaṅkaḷukku viti amittiṭa iyalum eṉṟu ippōtu teḷivāki varukiṟatu. avvāṟu ceyyiṉum vitikku etirāṉa coṟkaḷit tiruptiyāka viḷakka muṭiyumā eṉṟu ammuṟiyilum teḷivākavilli. viti piḻitta coṟkaḷ palavāṉāl potu viti māṟṟappaṭum. allatu ciṟappu vitikaḷiṉ eṇṇum mikutiyāka irukkum. vitikku etirāṉa colluruvaṅkaḷ cilavākiyāl avi puṟakkaṇikkappaṭum; ilakkaṇattiṟku avi oru poruṭṭalla eṉṟu otukkappaṭum.
oru moḻiyiṉ ellā uruvaṅkaḷiyum - vākkiyaṅkaḷ coṟkaḷ, olikkaṇaṅkaḷ aṉittiyum oru ilakkaṇam vivarittu uṟutippaṭutta iyalumā? avvāṟu ceyyum ilakkaṇam eṉṉa vakiyāṉa ilakkaṇam eṉṟu kūṟa muṭiyumā? vitikaḷaṭaṅkiya ilakkaṇam eṉṟā? allatu coṟpaṭṭiyal ilakkaṇam eṉṟā? attakiya coṟpaṭṭiyalkaḷik kaṇippoṟi tokuttut taruvatākak koṇṭālum, antac coṟpaṭṭiyalkaḷaṉittum oru ilakkaṇattil iṭam peṟumāṟu ceyya iyalumā? eṉpaṉa pōṉṟa kēḷvikaḷ eḻuvatu iyalpu.
ilakkaṇa amippu iyalpukaḷ paṟṟiya vivātamum viḷakkamum iṉṉum muṟṟuppeṟātatāl muṭinta muṭivāka evvita viḷakkamum antak kēḷvikaḷukku ippōtu aḷikka iyalātu. eṉiṉum oṉṟi maṭṭum uṟutiyākak kūṟa iyalum; palatarappaṭṭa colluruvaṅkaḷi uḷḷīṭāka iṭṭāl ilakkaṇattil potu vitikaḷ kuṟivākavum coṟpaṭṭiyal allatu ciṟappu vitikaḷ kūṭutalākavum kāṇappaṭum eṉpatu maṭṭum tiṭṭamākak kūṟa iyalum. maṟṟoru vakiyil kūṟiṉāl ilakkaṇattiṟkum akarātikkumuḷḷa vēṟupāṭu mikak kuṟivākavē irukkum.
marapuvaḻi ilakkaṇa āciriyarkaḷ, potu vitikaḷiṉ ceyaṟpāṭṭu vallamiyip perukka, utti oṉṟik kiyāṇṭu vitikaḷi viḷakkukiṉṟaṉar. ataṉ mūlam, perumaḷavil colluruvaṅkaḷi vitiyāl viḷakka muyaṉṟu veṟṟi kāṇkiṉṟaṉar. camakirutattil ataṉi `yukti eṉṟaḻippar. tolkāppiyar ataṉ pirivukaḷit tokuttuk kūṟiyuḷḷār. avar piṉ vanta yāpparuṅkāla viruttiyiṉ āciriyar ciṟiya māṟupāṭukaḷuṭaṉ avaṟṟit tokuttuḷḷār. naṉṉūlārum avaṟṟi varicippaṭuttiyuḷḷār. attakiya uttikaḷik kuṭilīyariṉ artta cāttiramum, āyurvēta nūlkaḷum tokuttuḷḷaṉa. caṭṭa nipuṇarkaḷ caṭṭatti viḷakkum pōtu oru viḷakka muṟiyip piṉpaṟṟuvatiyum nām iṅkē niṉivu kūrvatu naṉṟu. aracāṅka amippu muṟi pala ākiyāl, artta cāttiramum anta uttikaḷip piṉpaṟṟukiṉṟatu. nōykaḷum, nōy nīkkum maruntum palatarappaṭṭavi ākiyāl āyurvētamum avaṟṟip piṉpaṟṟukiṉṟatu. caṭṭa vitikaḷi uḷḷaṭakkum vaḻakkukaḷ kaṇakkaṟṟavi. ākiyāl vaḻakku maṉṟattil nītipatikaḷum vaḻakkaṟiñarkaḷum oruvakiyāṉa uttimuṟiyik kiyāṇṭu caṭṭavitikaḷi viḷakki virivākkukiṉṟaṉar. avaṟṟip pōṉṟē uḷḷīṭṭuc coṟkaḷ kaṇakkaṟṟaviyāka iruppatāl vitikaḷi utti mūlam virivākki ilakkaṇattil ceyalpaṭuttukiṉṟaṉar.
tolkāppiyam mutaliya ilakkaṇaṅkaḷ kūṟum uttikaḷip piṉ iṇippil avaṟṟiṉ poruḷuṭaṉ varicippaṭuttiyuḷḷēṉ. uttikaḷik kiyāṇṭu viḷakkam kūṟum vaḻikaḷ iruvakippaṭum. ilakkaṇam kūṟum vitikaḷil uḷḷa kuṟipāṭukaḷi nīkkap payaṉpaṭuttal 2. putiya colluruvaṅkaḷ avvappōtu tōṉṟumpōtu avaṟṟiyum vitikkuḷ aṭakkal ākiya iru muṉikaḷil uttikaḷip payaṉpaṭuttukiṉṟaṉar. ilakkaṇa vitikaḷ anta uttikaḷāl nirantara niliyip peṟukiṉṟaṉa. ataṉ mūlam ovvoru ilakkaṇamum kālattāl maṅkāta niliyip peṟukiṟatu. āṉāl anta uttikaḷip payaṉpaṭutti vitikaḷi virivākkuvatu allatu curukkuvatu pōṉṟa muyaṟcikaḷi uriyāciriyarkaḷtām ceytiṭa vēṇṭum. eṉavē anta viḷakkatti uriyāciriyarkaḷ cila muṟikaḷip piṉpaṟṟic ceykiṉṟaṉar. cintaṉi vaḷamuṭiya uriyāciriyarkaḷāl ōr ilakkaṇam, ataṉ mūlam, nirantaram peṟukiṉṟatu. virinta ceyaṟpāṭṭut tiṟamiyiyum peṟukiṉṟatu.
karuttaraṅkat tuvakkavuriyi āṟṟupavarkaḷ tamatu uric curukkattāl piṟar pārāṭṭip peṟum uttiyit terintu koḷvatu nallatu. pala amarvukaḷil ilakkaṇa amcaṅkaḷi virivāka ārāya irukkum uṅkaḷiṭam uttikaḷip paṟṟiya viḷakkattiyum, ilakkaṇattil avi peṟum iṭattiyum virivākak kūṟuvatu uṅkaḷ nēratti vīṇākkum eṉṟu añcukiṟēṉ. yārēṉum uttikaḷip paṟṟi iṅkē kēḷvi yeḻuppiṉāl piṉṉar nāṉ viḷakka muyalkiṟēṉ. marapu ilakkaṇaṅkaḷ aṉittum vitikaḷi ekkālattiṟkum ēṟṟaviyākap pōṟṟavē virumpukiṉṟaṉa. viṭupaṭṭa colluruvaṅkaḷi vitikkuḷ aṭakkuvataṟkum varuṅkālattil  paḻakkattil varum colluruvaṅkaḷit tām kūṟiya vitiyāl viḷakkavum muyalkiṉṟaṉa. ataṉāl urikārarkaḷum tamatu aṟivut tiṟamiyālum viḷakka āṟṟalālum mukkiya iṭamum matippum peṟukiṉṟaṉar. āṉāl avarkaḷ peṟum pārāṭṭu, cūttirakārari aṭuttuttāṉ. eṉavē marapu ilakkaṇaṅkaḷukku iru taḷaṅkaḷ uṇṭu. 1. cūttirakārariṉ ilakkaṇam 2. viruttiyurikārariṉ ilakkaṇam eṉpaviyē attaḷaṅkaḷ.
mēlē kūṟiya viḷakkaṅkaḷāl ellā marapu ilakkaṇaṅ kaḷiyum kālaṅkaṭantavi eṉṟu kūṟiviṭa muṭiyātu. āṉāl tiṟamiyāṉa viruttiyurikārariyuṭiya ilakkaṇam kālaṅ kaṭantu niṟkum; nirantaramāka māṟum taṉmi uṭiyatu eṉak kūṟa muṭiyum.
piṟcērkki
1. nutaliyatu aṟital: `cūttirattiṟkēṟpak karuti uṇarappaṭum poruḷ iṉṉateṉṟu koḷḷa vittal.
2. atikāra muṟimi: `muṉ cūttirap poruḷ piṉ cūttiraṅkaḷ perumpālāṉavaṟṟiṟkum peṟa vittal.
3. tokuttuk kūṟal: `tokuttu yātta nūlilum teḷiviṟkākat tokuttuk kūṟal.
4. vakuttu mey niṟuttal: `tokuttavaṟṟi vaki ceytu kūṟal; eṭuttukkāṭṭu, iṉam avi, valliṉam, melliṉam, iṭiyiṉam eṉṟal.
5. avvayiṉ moḻiyātataṉi moḻinta poruḷōṭu oṉṟa muṭṭiṉṟi muṭittal; `eṭuttōtiya poruṇmikku ēṟpa apporuḷil collāta oṉṟiyum koḷḷa vittal.
6. varātataṉi vantatu muṭittal: ilakkaṇam varāta cūttirattāṉ aṅṅaṉam vanta poruṇmikku vēṇṭum muṭivu koḷḷac ceytal.
7. vantatu koṇṭu vārātatuṇarttal `piṉṉoru vaḻi vantatu koṇṭu muṉ varātatōr poruḷaṟiya vittal.
8. muntu meḻintataṉ talitaṭumāṟṟam; `muṉkūṟiya muṟiyallāmal piṉṉoru kāl tali taṭumāṟṟamākak kūṟutal.
9. oppuk kūṟal: `oṉṟu kūṟumpōtu iruporuḷ kuṟittateṉṟu iraṭṭuṟac ceytal..
10. orutali moḻital: `oru atikārattil colla vēṇṭuvataṉi vēṟu atikārattil kāṇappaṭum ilakkaṇam poruntum eṉṟu aṅkē koḷḷa vittal.
11. taṉ kōṭ kūṟal: `collātaṉa palavāyiṉum annūlukku vēṇṭuvaṉavaṟṟi maṭṭum kūṟutal.
12. muṟi piṟaḻāmi: `cila poruḷik kāraṇamiṉṟi varicippaṭuttiya piṉṉar avvariciyiṉiyē piṉpaṟṟi ilakkaṇam kūṟal.
13. piṟaṉ uṭampaṭṭatu tāṉuṭampaṭutal; `vaḻakkil evvāṟu amikkappaṭṭuḷḷatō atip pōṉṟu tāṉum amittal.
14. ciṟantatu kāttal: `muṉ kūṟiya cūttirap poruḷip piṉṉoru cūttirattāl viḷakkutal.
15. etaratu pōṟṟal: `varukiṉṟa cūttirattiṉ poruḷukkēṟpa vēṟoru poruḷi muṉṉē kūṟutal.
16. moḻivāmeṉṟal; `oru payaṉ karuti muṉṉē kūṟuvōm eṉṟu collal.
17. kūṟiṟṟeṉṟal; muṉkūṟiya ilakkaṇatti maṟṟoru poruḷukkuk kūṟumpōtu mīṇṭum anta cūttirattik kūṟātu muṉkūṟiya cūttirattāṉ koḷḷutal.
18. taṉ kuṟiyiṭutal `ulakak kuṟiyiṉṟit taṉṉūluḷ vēṟu kuṟiyiṭṭu āḷal.
19. orutaliyiṉmi; `ellā iṭattum oruppōla vārātu, varum poḻutu aṅkē varuvatu eṉṟal.
20. muṭintatu kāṭṭal; `ellām viḷakkamākak kūṟātu, tollāciriyar kūṟiṉāreṉṟu collutal.
21. āṇi kūṟal: `ivvāciriyariṉ karuttu itutāṉ eṉṟu uṟutiyākak koḷḷa vittal.
22. palporuṭ kēṭpiṉ nallatu kōṭal: `pala poruḷil nallatik koḷḷal.
23. tokutta moḻiyāṉ vakuttaṉar kōṭal: `oru vāypāṭu eṭuttu ōtap pala vāypāṭu ataṟku vantu pūṇum eṉṟu vakuttukkoḷḷa vittal..
24. maṟutali citittut taṉ tuṇivu urittal: `oruvaṉ oru poruḷi vēṟupaṭak kūṟiṉ avvēṟupāṭṭiṉi māṟṟit tāṉ tuṇintavāṟu avaṉukkuk kūṟal.
25. piṟaṉkōḷ kūṟal: `taṉṉūl aṭippaṭiyil piṟa nūlukku ilakkaṇam koḷṟumāṟu kūṟal.
26. aṟiyātu uṭampaṭal: `tāṉ kūṟiya ilakkaṇattiṟku vēṟupaṭa varuvaṉa tāṉ aṟiyavilli eṉṟu kūṟi ataṉip puṟanaṭiyāk kūṟal.
27. poruḷiṭi iṭutal: `collukiṉṟa poruḷiṉ iṭiyē vēṟoru poruḷic collutalum collukiṉṟa poruḷukku ēṟṟa poruḷi āṇṭuc collātu piṟitoru vaḻic collutalum.
28. etirporuḷuṇarttal: muṉṉūṟkaḷāl ilakkaṇat tiṟku tiripu paṭum eṉṟu uṇarntu etirkālattiṟ kēṟṟatōr ilakkaṇam ceytal.
29. colliṉ eccam colliyāṅku uṇarttal; `colliṉ āṟṟalāl peṟappaṭum poruḷiyum eṭuttu ōtiya muṟiyil koḷḷa vittal.
30. tantu puṇarnturittal: `uḷporuḷallātataṉi uḷa pōlat tantu kūṭa uṇarttal.
31. ñāpakaṅ kūṟal: `curukkamākac collātu aritum peritumāka nalintu ceytu maṟṟataṉāṉē vēṟupala poruḷ uṇarttal.
32. uyttuk koṇṭuṇarttal: `oru poruḷic collumpōtu ataṉ kaṇṇē maṟṟoru poruḷik koṇarntu koṇṭaṟiyumeṉṟu tōṉṟac ceytal.
uttikaḷ muppattiraṇṭukkum mēṟpaṭṭavi eṉṟu
 nacciṉārkkiṉiyar uṇarntuḷḷatiyum iṅkē kuṟippiṭa vēṇṭum.
yāpparuṅkāla viruttiyil kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷa muppattiraṇṭu uttikaḷum pāṭalāṉār kūṟiyavi eṉṟu kuṟikkappaṭṭuḷḷatu eṉṟum, avarip piṉpaṟṟi navanītap pāṭṭiyal uriyilum ciṅkāra vaṭivēlu mutaliyār ceyta apitāṉa cintāmaṇiyilum kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa eṉṟum kēraḷap palkalikkaḻakat tamiḻt tuṟi āyvu māṇavar tiru. caṇmukam kūṟiṉār. pāṭalaṉār eṉṟa peyar pāṭaliyār eṉṟiruntatāl, artta cāttiram ceyta kuṭilīyar pāṭaliputtiraṉār ākiyāl avarik kuṟikkalām eṉṟu tōṉṟukiṟatu. artta cāttira uttikaḷuṭaṉ mēlē kuṟippiṭṭavaṟṟi iṉimēltāṉ oppunōkka vēṇṭum.
- va.ay. cupramaṇiyam kaṭṭurikaḷ,
pak. 49- 56
tokuppu: aruṇ cuppiramaṇiyaṉ
